| extensions/pi-claude-marketplace/orchestrators/plugin/info.messaging.ts:68:69 | PLUGIN_INFO_RENDER | status | unread | - | Kept as an outstanding finding by the orchestrators repair, after submitting a `type-selection` draft to the real engine against this tree and having it refused: `Invalid contract: extensions/pi-claude-marketplace/orchestrators/plugin/info.messaging.ts:68:69 filter extensions/pi-claude-marketplace/orchestrators/plugin/info.messaging.ts:68:37 selects over a type that does not discriminate on status`. Mechanism: `PluginInfoCascadeMsg` is `PluginSkippedMessage`, a single variant, so the prover's selected-over type is not a union and discriminates nothing. The draft was withdrawn, not rewritten. The filter was NOT dropped: every sibling render map in the family carries the same `Extract<Msg, { status: K }>` shape with an accepted entry, and dropping it here would hand each arm the whole union the moment info gains a second cascade status -- a widening the `as const satisfies` pin cannot catch. Correcting the product type to suit the prover is what this phase forbids. The gate now carries this row as a recorded decision in `scripts/check-unused-type-members.exceptions.json`, which names this exact coordinate, its owner and its key, and states the same mechanism; the member stays `unread` in this population and the recorded decision changes only the exit status. Removing the member, or letting its coordinates drift, makes that entry match nothing and refuses the run. |
| extensions/pi-claude-marketplace/orchestrators/plugin/shared.ts:129:5 | enableRowDependencies.signals | partition | unread | - | Kept as an outstanding finding by the orchestrators repair, deliberately and without touching the source. `LedgerDegradationSignals` declares no `partition`, and the `Pick<...>` left operand declares none either, so there is no slot to narrow and every restatement still ADDS a key -- the `type-refinement` prover's first check refuses it by construction, not by coordinate. The marker's documented job (WR-01) is to refuse `PluginUpdateUpdatedOutcome`, which declares the same two optional facts and would otherwise match structurally and silently return an empty dependency list for every update. Weakening the refusal to clear the row would reintroduce that bug. This is the row that keeps the mandatory gate blocked, and that is the truthful state. The gate now carries this row as a recorded decision in `scripts/check-unused-type-members.exceptions.json`, which names this exact coordinate, its owner and its key, and states the same mechanism; the member stays `unread` in this population and the recorded decision changes only the exit status. | extensions/pi-claude-marketplace/orchestrators/types.ts:155:3 | UpdatePhaseFailure | msg | unread | - | Kept as an outstanding finding by the orchestrators repair, and reclassified from the inherited disposition. The reader survey agrees with it -- the only syntax naming this declaration is the write at `update-swap.ts:929`, and the `f.msg` reads at `:921` and `:929` land on `Phase3Failure.msg` through `UpdatePhase3Failure`, a different declaration -- but the removal is still refused. `phaseFailures` is populated by exactly one path, the phase-3 rollback aggregation, and its own header states the slot's contract: surface the failures structurally 'so the cascade renderer can build the rollback-partial parent plus indented children block', with `notes[]` retained only for outcome-level text. A slot a rollback path populates is behaviour even when nothing reads it back today, so this is recorded rather than deleted. Deleting it would force a future consumer of the structured surface to re-parse the per-phase text out of `notes` prose.       "disposition": "explained",
      "note": "Kept as an outstanding finding by the orchestrators repair, after submitting a `type-selection` draft to the real engine against this tree and having it refused: `Invalid contract: extensions/pi-claude-marketplace/orchestrators/plugin/info.messaging.ts:68:69 filter extensions/pi-claude-marketplace/orchestrators/plugin/info.messaging.ts:68:37 selects over a type that does not discriminate on status`. Mechanism: `PluginInfoCascadeMsg` is `PluginSkippedMessage`, a single variant, so the prover's selected-over type is not a union and discriminates nothing. The draft was withdrawn, not rewritten. The filter was NOT dropped: every sibling render map in the family carries the same `Extract<Msg, { status: K }>` shape with an accepted entry, and dropping it here would hand each arm the whole union the moment info gains a second cascade status -- a widening the `as const satisfies` pin cannot catch. Correcting the product type to suit the prover is what this phase forbids. The gate now carries this row as a recorded decision in `scripts/check-unused-type-members.exceptions.json`, which names this exact coordinate, its owner and its key, and states the same mechanism; the member stays `unread` in this population and the recorded decision changes only the exit status. Removing the member, or letting its coordinates drift, makes that entry match nothing and refuses the run."

      "note": "Kept as an outstanding finding by the orchestrators repair, deliberately and without touching the source. `LedgerDegradationSignals` declares no `partition`, and the `Pick<...>` left operand declares none either, so there is no slot to narrow and every restatement still ADDS a key -- the `type-refinement` prover's first check refuses it by construction, not by coordinate.     {
      "id": "extensions/pi-claude-marketplace/orchestrators/types.ts:155:3",
      "path": "extensions/pi-claude-marketplace/orchestrators/types.ts",
      "owner": "UpdatePhaseFailure",
      "key": "msg",
      "status": "unread",
      "disposition": "explained",
      "note": "Kept as an outstanding finding by the orchestrators repair, and reclassified from the inherited disposition. The reader survey agrees with it -- the only syntax naming this declaration is the write at `update-swap.ts:929`, and the `f.msg` reads at `:921` and `:929` land on `Phase3Failure.msg` through `UpdatePhase3Failure`, a different declaration -- but the removal is still refused. `phaseFailures` is populated by exactly one path, the phase-3 rollback aggregation, and its own header states the slot's contract: surface the failures structurally 'so the cascade renderer can build the rollback-partial parent plus indented children block', with `notes[]` retained only for outcome-level text. A slot a rollback path populates is behaviour even when nothing reads it back today, so this is recorded rather than deleted. Deleting it would force a future consumer of the structured surface to re-parse the per-phase text out of `notes` prose. The gate now carries this row as a recorded decision in `scripts/check-unused-type-members.exceptions.json`, which names this exact coordinate, its owner and its key, and states the same mechanism; the member stays `unread` in this population and the recorded decision changes only the exit status. Removing the member, or letting its coordinates drift, makes that entry match nothing and refuses the run."
    },
